샷 0518–24초 · 6초 · LOSS — one goes in

구도 중경: 주인공 옆의 다른 B-26. 배경: 아카기의 선체와 항적.

  1. 18–19.5초 다른 B-26의 오른쪽 엔진이 대공탄에 맞아 터지고 불길이 날개를 덮는다.
  2. 19.5–21초 날개가 꺾이듯 내려가고 기체가 옆으로 미끄러진다.
  3. 21–22.5초 날개 끝이 물을 잡아채 기체가 수레바퀴처럼 돌며 부서진다.
  4. 22.5–24초 거대한 흰 물기둥과 검은 연기가 솟고 파편이 쏟아진다.

효과·물리 파편은 다시 날지 않는다. 물기둥은 중력대로 무너진다.

카메라 75mm, 주인공 기체에서 본 듯한 측면 추적.

빛 주황 화염, 흰 물, 검은 연기.

소리 폭발, 금속 비명, 물 충돌음.

추락기 우→좌로 끌려감.

마지막 프레임 가라앉는 물기둥.

참고 @image1 · @image2 · @image5

샷 0624–30초 · 6초 · HERO COCKPIT — hold it steady

구도 B-26 조종석 정면 사선. 조종사 왼쪽, 부조종사 오른쪽. 앞 유리 너머 아카기가 점점 커진다.

  1. 24–26초 탄이 앞 유리 모서리에 맞아 별 모양 금이 간다. 둘 다 움찔한다.
  2. 26–28초 조종사가 턱에 힘을 주고 조종간을 두 손으로 버틴다. 부조종사는 계기판 손잡이를 움켜쥔다.
  3. 28–30초 앞 유리 가득 아카기의 선체가 들어찬다.

연기 공포를 삼키는 집중. 누구도 소리 지르지 않는다.

카메라 50mm, 조종석 앞 바깥쪽에서, 기체 진동.

빛 앞 유리 금 사이로 갈라지는 햇빛, 대공 섬광.

소리 유리 깨지는 소리, 엔진, 탄이 동체를 치는 소리.

미군 얼굴 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 앞 유리를 채운 아카기.

참고 @image3 · @image2 · @image5

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • B-26 조종사 — 30세. 길고 마른 얼굴, 가는 연필 콧수염, 담갈색 눈, 흰 피부, 가죽 비행모·이마의 고글.
  • B-26 부조종사 — 25세. 둥근 얼굴, 검은 머리, 붙은 듯 굵은 눈썹, 짙은 갈색 눈, 부드러운 턱.
  • 일본 대공포 사수 — 21세. 마른 얼굴, 날카로운 턱, 철모 아래 삭발, 악문 이, 흰 러닝셔츠. 연장 기관포 조준석.
  • 일본 대공포 급탄수 — 19세. 둥근 앳된 얼굴, 짧은 삭발, 넓은 미간, 상기된 볼, 목의 흰 수건. 15발 상자 탄창 장전.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

B-26 4대 중 1대가 이 클립에서 추락. 주인공 B-26(조종사·부조종사)은 손상 없이 계속. 일본 대공포는 연장 25mm, 사수+급탄수가 실제로 운용.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (9,178자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Mid-morning: high sun, sea glare, black flak and white shell splashes everywhere.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight
@image3 [ASSET] US B-26 pilot flight gear, helmet, life vest
@image4 [ASSET] Kagero-class destroyer: hull, two funnels, gun mounts
@image5 [ASSET] carrier Akagi: hull, hinomaru on bow, island on PORT side, downward-curved funnel on STARBOARD side
@image6 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear
@image8 [ASSET] A6M2 Zero, grey-green, red hinomaru, wheels retracted, no drop tank
@image9 [ASSET] US Mark 13 aerial torpedo: long body, nose, tail, mount

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
B-26 PILOT — American Army pilot, 30. Long lean face, thin pencil mustache, hazel eyes, fair skin, brown hair under a leather flight helmet, goggles up, sweat at the temples.
B-26 CO-PILOT — American, 25. Round face, black hair, thick joined eyebrows, dark-brown eyes, soft rounded chin, headset over his flight helmet.
JAPANESE AA GUNNER — Japanese sailor, 21. Thin face, sharp jaw, shaved head under a steel helmet with chin strap, gritted teeth, white undershirt, sweat; sits in the pointer seat of a twin 25mm mount.
JAPANESE AA LOADER — Japanese sailor, 19. Round boyish face, near-shaved head, wide-set eyes, flushed cheeks, white towel around his neck, slams 15-round box magazines onto the gun.
Americans: varied European-American ancestry (Irish, Italian, German, Polish), varied hair colour, deeper-set eyes, higher nose bridges, forearm hair, sunburnt red-pink skin, stubble, sweat. Real 1942 faces — no movie-star polish.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
The B-26s fly BELOW the destroyer's deck height, close beside the hull — not far away. Japanese guns are fired by visible men. Only one B-26 crashes in this clip.

[STRUCTURE]
30s, 6 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: just above the waves; beyond a B-26 seen from behind, a Japanese destroyer and Akagi grow fast; propwash rips spray lines across the surface.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–4s) — WAVE-TOP — under their guns
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image4, @image5
Frame: Low behind a B-26's tail. Foreground: tailplane and rear fuselage. Midground: the fuselage 10 metres above the waves. Background: a destroyer (@image4) growing fast, Akagi (@image5) beyond.
Action:
- 0–2s: the B-26 levels at 10 metres; propwash tears twin spray lines across the water
- 2–4s: the destroyer's side guns flash together; white columns erupt from the sea ahead
Camera: chase behind the tail, near the water, slight shake.
Light: hard sea glare, backlit spray.
Sound: twin engines, wind tearing at the waves, distant gunfire.
Ends on: the B-26 boring in between the columns.

Shot 2 (4–9s) · CUT TO — THE GUNNERS — Japan fires back
Refs: @image6, @image7, @image4
Frame: A twin 25mm mount (@image6) on the destroyer's deck: the AA GUNNER in the pointer seat left, the AA LOADER right with a 15-round box magazine. Foreground: piling brass, the rail. Background: B-26s and flak in the sky.
Action:
- 4–5.5s: the gunner presses into the shoulder rests and cranks the twin barrels low to the right
- 5.5–7.5s: the barrels fire alternately; brass rains onto the deck
- 7.5–9s: the loader rips off the empty magazine, tosses it, and slams a fresh one down on top
Acting: the gunner grits his teeth, one eye shut; the loader is terrified but his hands never stop.
Camera: 40mm low beside the mount, a jolt on every shot.
Light: faces strobing in muzzle flash, white sky.
Sound: rapid twin-cannon bursts, ringing brass, wordless shouts.
Ends on: the fresh magazine seated, fire continuing.

Shot 3 (9–13s) · CUT TO — RING SIGHT — the target grows
Refs: @image6, @image2, @image1
Frame: Gunner's POV. Foreground: a concentric ring sight, sharp; the two barrels soft on either side. Inside the rings: a B-26 low over the sea.
Action:
- 9–11s: the B-26 slides from the edge of the rings toward the centre, growing
- 11–13s: tracer streams arc from the muzzles toward the B-26 in the rings
Camera: ring-sight POV, small hand-wheel tracking.
Light: bright sky, black rings.
Sound: the bursts, the gunner's hard breath.
Ends on: the B-26 centred in the rings.

Shot 4 (13–18s) · CUT TO — GAUNTLET — beside the hull
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image4
Frame: Sea level. Left half of frame: the destroyer's hull side as a wall — rivets, rust streaks, running water. A B-26 passes right-to-left close beside it, LOWER than the deck. Sky: black flak.
Action:
- 13–15s: the B-26 roars past a few dozen metres from the hull, below deck level
- 15–16.5s: crew at the rail duck; muzzles depress downward
- 16.5–18s: a line of cannon splashes stitches the water under its wingtip
Camera: almost fixed 1 metre above the water, fast pan with the B-26.
Light: hard contrast between hull shadow and bright sky.
Sound: engine Doppler, the roar echoing off the hull, splashes.
Ends on: the B-26 clearing the hull ahead.

Shot 5 (18–24s) · CUT TO — LOSS — one goes in
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image5
Frame: Midground: a different B-26 beside the hero. Background: Akagi's hull and wake.
Action:
- 18–19.5s: another B-26's right engine bursts under AA hits; fire sheets over the wing
- 19.5–21s: the wing drops and the aircraft slides sideways
- 21–22.5s: the wingtip catches the sea; the aircraft cartwheels and breaks apart
- 22.5–24s: a huge white column and black smoke rise; debris falls
Camera: 75mm side tracking, as if from the hero aircraft.
Light: orange fire, white water, black smoke.
FX: debris never flies back up; the column collapses under gravity.
Sound: explosion, shrieking metal, the slam of water.
Ends on: the column collapsing.

Shot 6 (24–30s) · CUT TO — HERO COCKPIT — hold it steady
Refs: @image3, @image2, @image5
Frame: B-26 cockpit, front three-quarter: PILOT left, CO-PILOT right; Akagi growing beyond the windscreen.
Action:
- 24–26s: a round stars the corner of the windscreen; both flinch
- 26–28s: the PILOT clenches his jaw and holds the yoke with both hands; the CO-PILOT grips the panel handle
- 28–30s: Akagi's hull fills the windscreen
Acting: fear swallowed into focus; no one screams.
Camera: 50mm from outside the nose, airframe vibration.
Light: sunlight splintering through the cracks, flak flashes.
Sound: glass crack, engines, rounds pinging the fuselage.
Ends on: Akagi filling the windscreen.

[AUDIO]
Speak only the quoted lines, once each, in the stated language, at the stated moment. Diegetic sound only — wind, sea, engines, guns, metal, wordless voices — carried across cuts. No music, no narration.

C04 · 편집 위치 02:48–03:16

“Release!” · 함선 옆에 떨어지는 어뢰

28초 · 6샷 · 첨부 8장 · 대사 1

주인공 B-26이 아카기 바로 옆에서 어뢰를 떨어뜨리고 갑판 위를 스치듯 넘어간다. 뒤따르던 B-26은 불붙은 채 함교 옆을 스쳐 바다에 처박힌다. 일본 포수들은 쉬지 않고 쏜다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C03의 앞 유리를 채운 아카기에서 바로 잇는다.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
곧은 어뢰 항적이 아카기를 향해 달린다. 다음 클립에서 아카기가 방향을 튼다.

첫 화면
B-26 조종석. 조종사의 엄지가 빨간 투하 스위치 위에 있고, 앞 유리 너머 아카기 선체가 가득하다.

샷별 연출 6개 · 초 단위 동작
샷 010–4초 · 4초 · RELEASE

구도 조종사 오른손과 투하 스위치 인서트에서 얼굴로. 앞 유리 너머 아카기.

  1. 0–1초 장갑 낀 엄지가 빨간 덮개를 튕겨 연다.
  2. 1–2.5초 조종사가 외친다.
  3. 2.5–4초 엄지가 스위치를 한 번 누르고, 기체가 가벼워지며 위로 살짝 뜬다.

B-26 조종사 · 1–2.5초 · Release! (투하! (대본: 발사!))

카메라 인서트 100mm 매크로 → 65mm 얼굴로 컷 없이 패닝.

빛 계기판 그늘, 앞 유리의 강한 빛.

소리 스위치 딸깍, 짧은 외침, 투하 쇳소리.

미군 얼굴 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 살짝 뜨는 기체에 몸이 눌리는 조종사.

참고 @image3 · @image4

샷 024–9초 · 5초 · DROP — beside the carrier

구도 B-26 배 아래 측면. 전경: 해면. 중경: B-26 배와 어뢰. 배경: 수백 미터 앞 아카기 선체가 화면 오른쪽 절반을 채운다.

  1. 4–5.5초 어뢰가 거치대에서 분리되어 기수를 살짝 숙인 채 떨어진다.
  2. 5.5–7초 얕게 물을 때리며 흰 물보라를 튀기고 사라진다.
  3. 7–9초 B-26은 빈 거치대를 보이며 계속 왼쪽으로 가고, 수면 아래로 곧은 기포 항적이 아카기 쪽으로 뻗는다.

카메라 해면 위 2m 측면 추적 후 항적에 머문다.

빛 해면 반사, 흰 물보라.

소리 어뢰 이탈음, 물 때리는 소리, 엔진.

B-26 우→좌 / 어뢰 항적 아카기 방향.

마지막 프레임 빈 거치대와 뻗어 가는 항적.

참고 @image2 · @image5 · @image1 · @image6

샷 039–14초 · 5초 · OVER THE DECK

구도 아카기 비행갑판 위, 섬 근처 높은 위치. 전경: 섬 옆 대공포좌 난간. 중경: 비행갑판(나무 결, 대기 중인 기체). 오른쪽에서 B-26이 갑판 위를 낮게 가로지른다.

  1. 9–11초 B-26이 기수를 들어 비행갑판 바로 위를 스친다. 갑판 승무원들이 바닥에 엎드린다.
  2. 11–12.5초 섬 옆 포좌의 포신들이 B-26을 따라 돌며 거의 자기 갑판을 향해 쏜다.
  3. 12.5–14초 B-26이 반대편 바다로 빠져나가고 프로펠러 바람에 갑판 깃발이 찢어질 듯 펄럭인다.

카메라 35mm, 섬 위에서 B-26을 따라 빠른 팬.

빛 갑판의 강한 반사, 연기의 그림자.

소리 머리 위를 찢는 쌍발 엔진, 대공포, 고함.

B-26 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 빠져나가는 B-26 꼬리.

참고 @image2 · @image6 · @image7

샷 0414–19초 · 5초 · THE BURNING ONE

구도 아카기 선체 외경, 수면 높이. 화면 오른쪽: 선체와 섬. 오른쪽 위에서 왼쪽 아래로: 불붙은 두 번째 B-26.

  1. 14–15.5초 두 번째 B-26이 왼쪽 날개 뿌리에서 불길을 끌며 섬 높이로 날아든다.
  2. 15.5–17초 섬 옆을 몇 미터 차이로 스치고 승무원들이 몸을 숙인다.
  3. 17–19초 선체 너머 바다에 처박혀 거대한 불기둥과 물기둥이 솟는다.

효과·물리 불은 날개 뿌리에서만, 연기는 바람 따라 오른쪽.

카메라 수면 위 낮은 위치, 기체를 따라 팬 후 충돌에서 고정.

빛 주황 화염과 검은 연기, 회청색 선체.

소리 불타는 엔진의 절규, 충돌 폭음, 물 쏟아지는 소리.

추락기 우→좌 사선 아래.

마지막 프레임 선체 옆에서 무너지는 물기둥.

참고 @image2 · @image6 · @image1

샷 0519–23초 · 4초 · NO PAUSE — Japanese gunners

구도 아카기 측면 포좌 클로즈업. 사수와 급탄수, 뒤로 연기 낀 하늘.

  1. 19–20.5초 사수가 소리 지르며 남은 기체를 쫓아 포를 돌린다.
  2. 20.5–22초 급탄수가 새 탄창을 내리꽂고 뜨거운 탄피에 손을 데어 움찔한다.
  3. 22–23초 탄피 더미가 발목까지 쌓인다.

카메라 40mm 핸드헬드, 반동에 흔들림.

빛 섬광, 연기에 가린 해.

소리 연사, 단어 없는 고함, 탄피.

일본 얼굴 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 멈추지 않는 포.

참고 @image7 · @image8

샷 0623–28초 · 5초 · THE WAKE RUNS

구도 높은 사선. 전경 아래: 곧은 기포 항적. 배경: 아카기 선체.

  1. 23–25.5초 어뢰 항적이 아카기 옆구리를 향해 곧게 달린다.
  2. 25.5–28초 아카기 함수가 하얗게 물을 가르며 막 돌기 시작한다.

카메라 높은 사선에서 항적을 따라 느린 푸시.

빛 반짝이는 해면 위 흰 기포 선.

소리 물살, 먼 포성, 아카기 기관의 진동.

항적 우→좌, 아카기 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 항적과 돌기 시작한 선수.

참고 @image1 · @image5 · @image6

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • B-26 조종사 — 30세. 길고 마른 얼굴, 가는 연필 콧수염, 담갈색 눈, 흰 피부, 가죽 비행모·이마의 고글.
  • B-26 부조종사 — 25세. 둥근 얼굴, 검은 머리, 붙은 듯 굵은 눈썹, 짙은 갈색 눈, 부드러운 턱.
  • 일본 대공포 사수 — 21세. 마른 얼굴, 날카로운 턱, 철모 아래 삭발, 악문 이, 흰 러닝셔츠. 연장 기관포 조준석.
  • 일본 대공포 급탄수 — 19세. 둥근 앳된 얼굴, 짧은 삭발, 넓은 미간, 상기된 볼, 목의 흰 수건. 15발 상자 탄창 장전.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

주인공 B-26: 어뢰 투하 → 빈 거치대 → 갑판 위 통과 후 이탈(생존). 두 번째 B-26: 화재 → 추락. 어뢰는 아카기 쪽으로 항주.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (8,668자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Mid-morning: high sun, sea glare, flak, smoke drifting across Akagi's deck.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight
@image3 [ASSET] US B-26 pilot flight gear, helmet, life vest
@image4 [ASSET] B-26 cockpit: side-by-side seats, analogue gauges, release switch, canopy frame
@image5 [ASSET] US Mark 13 aerial torpedo: long body, nose, tail, mount
@image6 [ASSET] carrier Akagi: hull, hinomaru on bow, island on PORT side, downward-curved funnel on STARBOARD side
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image8 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
B-26 PILOT — American Army pilot, 30. Long lean face, thin pencil mustache, hazel eyes, fair skin, brown hair under a leather flight helmet, goggles up, sweat at the temples.
B-26 CO-PILOT — American, 25. Round face, black hair, thick joined eyebrows, dark-brown eyes, soft rounded chin, headset over his flight helmet.
JAPANESE AA GUNNER — Japanese sailor, 21. Thin face, sharp jaw, shaved head under a steel helmet with chin strap, gritted teeth, white undershirt, sweat; sits in the pointer seat of a twin 25mm mount.
JAPANESE AA LOADER — Japanese sailor, 19. Round boyish face, near-shaved head, wide-set eyes, flushed cheeks, white towel around his neck, slams 15-round box magazines onto the gun.
Americans: varied European-American ancestry (Irish, Italian, German, Polish), varied hair colour, deeper-set eyes, higher nose bridges, forearm hair, sunburnt red-pink skin, stubble, sweat. Real 1942 faces — no movie-star polish.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
Exactly one torpedo leaves the hero B-26 and the rack stays empty afterwards. The torpedo drops close beside the carrier, not far away. Akagi's island is on her PORT side. No hit on Akagi in this clip.

[STRUCTURE]
28s, 6 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: B-26 cockpit: the PILOT's thumb over the red release switch; Akagi's hull fills the windscreen.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–4s) — RELEASE
Refs: @image3, @image4
Frame: Insert of the PILOT's right hand on the release switch, then his face; Akagi beyond the windscreen.
Action:
- 0–1s: a gloved thumb flips the red guard open
- 1–2.5s: the PILOT shouts
- 2.5–4s: the thumb presses once; the aircraft, suddenly lighter, lifts slightly
Dialogue (B-26 PILOT, English, barked) at 1–2.5s: "Release!"
Camera: 100mm macro insert, then a whip pan to his face at 65mm.
Light: panel shadow, hard light through the windscreen.
Sound: switch click, the shout, the clank of release.
Ends on: the PILOT pressed into his seat as the aircraft lifts.

Shot 2 (4–9s) · CUT TO — DROP — beside the carrier
Refs: @image2, @image5, @image1, @image6
Frame: Low side view under the B-26. Foreground: the sea surface. Midground: the belly and the torpedo (@image5). Background: Akagi's hull, a few hundred metres off, filling the right half.
Action:
- 4–5.5s: the torpedo separates from the rack and falls, nose slightly down
- 5.5–7s: it slaps into the water shallow, throws white spray, and disappears
- 7–9s: the B-26 continues left showing the empty rack; a straight bubble wake runs under the surface toward Akagi
Camera: side tracking 2 metres above the water, then hold on the wake.
Light: sea glare, white spray.
Sound: release clank, the slap of water, engines.
Ends on: the empty rack and the running wake.

Shot 3 (9–14s) · CUT TO — OVER THE DECK
Refs: @image2, @image6, @image7
Frame: High beside Akagi's island. Foreground: an AA tub rail by the island. Midground: the flight deck — wood planking, parked aircraft. A B-26 crosses low over the deck from the right.
Action:
- 9–11s: the B-26 pulls up and skims just over the flight deck; deck crew throw themselves flat
- 11–12.5s: barrels in the island tub swing after it, firing almost across their own deck
- 12.5–14s: the B-26 clears the far side; its propwash whips the deck flags
Camera: 35mm from the island, fast pan with the B-26.
Light: hard deck glare, drifting smoke shadows.
Sound: twin engines tearing overhead, AA, shouts.
Ends on: the B-26's tail escaping.

Shot 4 (14–19s) · CUT TO — THE BURNING ONE
Refs: @image2, @image6, @image1
Frame: Akagi's hull from sea level. Frame right: hull and island. From upper right to lower left: a second B-26, on fire.
Action:
- 14–15.5s: a second B-26 comes in at island height trailing fire from its left wing root
- 15.5–17s: it misses the island by metres; crewmen duck
- 17–19s: it slams into the sea beyond the hull; a huge column of fire and water rises
Camera: low above the water, pan with the aircraft, then hold on impact.
Light: orange fire and black smoke against the blue-grey hull.
FX: fire only from the wing root; smoke drifting right with the wind.
Sound: the screaming burning engine, the impact, water crashing down.
Ends on: the column collapsing beside the hull.

Shot 5 (19–23s) · CUT TO — NO PAUSE — Japanese gunners
Refs: @image7, @image8
Frame: Close on an AA mount on Akagi's side: the AA GUNNER and AA LOADER, smoky sky behind.
Action:
- 19–20.5s: the gunner yells, swinging the mount after the remaining aircraft
- 20.5–22s: the loader slams a fresh magazine, flinching as hot brass burns his hand
- 22–23s: brass piles up to their ankles
Camera: 40mm handheld, recoil shake.
Light: muzzle flash, smoke-dimmed sun.
Sound: bursts, wordless yelling, brass.
Ends on: the gun never stops.

Shot 6 (23–28s) · CUT TO — THE WAKE RUNS
Refs: @image1, @image5, @image6
Frame: High oblique. Foreground: the straight bubble wake. Background: Akagi's hull.
Action:
- 23–25.5s: the torpedo wake runs straight at Akagi's side
- 25.5–28s: Akagi's bow throws white water as she just begins to turn
Camera: high oblique, slow push along the wake.
Light: white bubble line on the glittering sea.
Sound: rushing water, distant guns, Akagi's machinery.
Ends on: the wake and the turning bow.

[AUDIO]
Speak only the quoted lines, once each, in the stated language, at the stated moment. Diegetic sound only — wind, sea, engines, guns, metal, wordless voices — carried across cuts. No music, no narration.

C05 · 편집 위치 03:16–03:42

방향을 트는 아카기 · 옆으로 흐르는 어뢰

26초 · 5샷 · 첨부 8장 · 대사 0

아카기가 급선회해 어뢰 항적들이 선체 옆과 함미 뒤 빈 물로 흘러간다. 상공에서는 제로센이 이탈하는 미군기를 쫓고, 함대의 포는 멈추지 않는다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C04의 달리는 항적과 돌기 시작한 선수를 잇는다.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
두 번째 어뢰가 함미 뒤 빈 물로 사라지고, 아카기는 대공 사격 속에 자세를 바로잡는다.

첫 화면
높은 부감. 아카기가 크게 기울며 하얀 원호를 그리고, 곧은 어뢰 항적 두 줄이 그 원호 안쪽으로 뻗어 온다.

번호순 첨부 이미지 8장 · 기존 에셋 그대로

샷별 연출 5개 · 초 단위 동작
샷 010–6초 · 6초 · HARD TURN

구도 높은 사선 부감. 화면 가운데: 기울어진 아카기와 하얀 원호 항적. 아래 왼쪽→오른쪽 위: 곧은 어뢰 항적 두 줄. 위쪽: 제로센 두 대가 가로지른다.

  1. 0–2초 아카기가 크게 기울며 원호를 그린다. 비행갑판 위 기체들이 줄에 묶인 채 흔들린다.
  2. 2–4초 어뢰 항적 두 줄이 원호 안쪽으로 곧게 뻗어 온다.
  3. 4–6초 제로센 두 대가 화면 위를 좌→우로 가로지르고 주변 해면에 근탄 물기둥이 솟는다.

카메라 높은 부감, 아카기 선회를 따라 느린 회전.

빛 해면의 반짝임, 흰 원호.

소리 멀리서 들리는 기관 진동, 포성, 제로센 엔진.

아카기 좌→우로 선회.

마지막 프레임 원호 안으로 다가오는 항적.

참고 @image1 · @image2 · @image4 · @image5

샷 026–11초 · 5초 · MISS — alongside

구도 수면 높이. 화면 위 절반: 아카기 선체 측면(물보라 젖음). 아래 절반: 해면. 오른쪽→왼쪽: 선체와 나란히 흐르는 어뢰 기포 항적.

  1. 6–8초 어뢰 항적이 선체와 몇 미터 간격으로 나란히 달린다.
  2. 8–10초 선체가 계속 돌면서 간격이 벌어진다.
  3. 10–11초 항적이 함미를 지나 빈 물로 사라진다.

카메라 수면 위 50cm, 항적을 따라 측면 트래킹.

빛 선체의 짙은 그늘, 기포의 흰 반짝임.

소리 물살, 선체를 때리는 파도, 어뢰 프로펠러의 웅웅거림.

항적 선체와 평행.

마지막 프레임 빈 물만 남은 함미 뒤.

참고 @image1 · @image2 · @image4

샷 0311–15초 · 4초 · ON DECK — hold on

구도 섬에서 내려다본 비행갑판. 기울어진 갑판, 붙잡는 승무원들, 옆 포좌의 사격.

  1. 11–13초 갑판이 기울고 승무원들이 줄과 난간을 붙잡는다.
  2. 13–15초 현측 포좌의 사수와 급탄수가 기울어진 채 계속 쏜다.

카메라 35mm, 섬 난간에서 내려다봄, 선체 기울기 그대로.

빛 강한 해, 연기 그림자.

소리 바람, 기관포, 고함.

일본 포구 오른쪽 위.

마지막 프레임 기울어진 갑판의 사격.

참고 @image2 · @image7 · @image8

샷 0415–20초 · 5초 · CROSSING — the hunters

구도 하늘 낮은 고도. 아래: 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 이탈하는 B-26. 위 왼쪽: 내려오며 사격하는 제로센.

  1. 15–17초 B-26이 연기를 끌며 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 낮게 이탈한다.
  2. 17–19초 제로센이 왼쪽 위에서 오른쪽 아래로 내리꽂히며 사격, 예광탄이 B-26 꼬리 옆을 스친다.
  3. 19–20초 두 기체가 교차해 서로 반대쪽으로 빠진다.

카메라 공중 측면 추적, 교차 순간 짧은 휩 팬.

빛 역광, 연기의 실루엣.

소리 두 종류 엔진의 교차, 기관포.

제로센 좌→우 / B-26 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 서로 멀어지는 두 기체.

참고 @image5 · @image6 · @image1

샷 0520–26초 · 6초 · STEADY — but not safe

구도 함미 뒤 낮은 위치. 전경: 항적의 흰 거품. 중경: 자세를 바로잡는 아카기. 오른쪽: 사격 중인 구축함.

  1. 20–22초 두 번째 어뢰 항적이 함미 뒤 빈 물로 지나간다.
  2. 22–24초 아카기가 기울기를 회복하고 갑판이 수평으로 돌아온다.
  3. 24–26초 주변 구축함과 아카기의 포들이 여전히 하늘을 향해 불을 뿜고 대공 연기가 갑판 위로 흘러간다.

카메라 수면 가까이 고정에 가까운 와이드, 느린 상승.

빛 흰 거품, 회청색 선체, 검은 연기.

소리 기관, 물살, 이어지는 포성.

아카기 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 포연 속 자세를 바로잡은 아카기.

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • 일본 대공포 사수 — 21세. 마른 얼굴, 날카로운 턱, 철모 아래 삭발, 악문 이, 흰 러닝셔츠. 연장 기관포 조준석.
  • 일본 대공포 급탄수 — 19세. 둥근 앳된 얼굴, 짧은 삭발, 넓은 미간, 상기된 볼, 목의 흰 수건. 15발 상자 탄창 장전.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

어뢰 명중 없음. 항적은 선체 옆과 함미 뒤를 지나간다. 제로센 다수, 이탈하는 B-26. 함교 내부 장면 없음(대본 삭제).

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (7,333자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Mid-morning: high sun, white wakes, flak and smoke over the fleet.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] carrier Akagi: hull, hinomaru on bow, island on PORT side, downward-curved funnel on STARBOARD side
@image3 [ASSET] Kagero-class destroyer: hull, two funnels, gun mounts
@image4 [ASSET] US Mark 13 aerial torpedo: long body, nose, tail, mount
@image5 [ASSET] A6M2 Zero, grey-green, red hinomaru, wheels retracted, no drop tank
@image6 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image8 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
JAPANESE AA GUNNER — Japanese sailor, 21. Thin face, sharp jaw, shaved head under a steel helmet with chin strap, gritted teeth, white undershirt, sweat; sits in the pointer seat of a twin 25mm mount.
JAPANESE AA LOADER — Japanese sailor, 19. Round boyish face, near-shaved head, wide-set eyes, flushed cheeks, white towel around his neck, slams 15-round box magazines onto the gun.
Americans: varied European-American ancestry (Irish, Italian, German, Polish), varied hair colour, deeper-set eyes, higher nose bridges, forearm hair, sunburnt red-pink skin, stubble, sweat. Real 1942 faces — no movie-star polish.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
No torpedo hits Akagi. No crashing aircraft in the destroyer shots. Akagi's island stays on her PORT side.

[STRUCTURE]
26s, 5 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: high aerial: Akagi heels into a hard turn drawing a white arc; two straight torpedo wakes reach in toward the arc.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–6s) — HARD TURN
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image4, @image5
Frame: High oblique aerial. Centre: Akagi heeled over, a white arc of wake. Two straight torpedo wakes reach in from the lower edge. Above: two Zeros crossing.
Action:
- 0–2s: Akagi heels hard, carving an arc; parked aircraft strain against their tie-downs
- 2–4s: two straight torpedo wakes reach in toward the arc
- 4–6s: two Zeros streak left-to-right across the top; near-miss columns rise around her
Camera: high aerial, slow orbit following the turn.
Light: glittering sea, the white arc.
Sound: distant machinery, gunfire, Zero engines.
Ends on: wakes closing on the arc.

Shot 2 (6–11s) · CUT TO — MISS — alongside
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image4
Frame: Water level. Upper half: Akagi's hull side, wet with spray. Lower half: the sea. A torpedo's bubble wake runs parallel to the hull.
Action:
- 6–8s: the wake runs parallel to the hull a few metres off
- 8–10s: the hull keeps turning; the gap widens
- 10–11s: the wake passes the stern into empty water
Camera: 50cm above the water, lateral tracking with the wake.
Light: deep hull shadow, white glint of bubbles.
Sound: rushing water, waves slapping the hull, the torpedo's propeller hum.
Ends on: empty water behind the stern.

Shot 3 (11–15s) · CUT TO — ON DECK — hold on
Refs: @image2, @image7, @image8
Frame: From the island looking down the tilted flight deck: crew hanging on, the side tub firing.
Action:
- 11–13s: the deck tilts; crew grab lines and rails
- 13–15s: the AA GUNNER and AA LOADER keep firing while canted over
Camera: 35mm down from the island rail, the ship's heel kept in frame.
Light: hard sun, smoke shadows.
Sound: wind, cannon, shouts.
Ends on: firing from a canted deck.

Shot 4 (15–20s) · CUT TO — CROSSING — the hunters
Refs: @image5, @image6, @image1
Frame: Low sky. Below: a B-26 escaping right-to-left. Upper left: a Zero diving and firing.
Action:
- 15–17s: a B-26 escapes low right-to-left, trailing smoke
- 17–19s: a Zero dives from upper left to lower right, firing; tracers flash past the B-26's tail
- 19–20s: the two cross and part in opposite directions
Camera: aerial side tracking, a short whip pan at the cross.
Light: backlight, smoke silhouettes.
Sound: two engine notes crossing, cannon.
Ends on: the two aircraft separating.

Shot 5 (20–26s) · CUT TO — STEADY — but not safe
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image3, @image7
Frame: Low behind the stern. Foreground: white wake foam. Midground: Akagi righting herself. Right: a destroyer firing.
Action:
- 20–22s: the second torpedo wake slides past behind the stern into empty water
- 22–24s: Akagi recovers her heel; the deck comes level
- 24–26s: her guns and the destroyer's keep firing skyward; flak smoke drifts over the deck
Camera: near-fixed wide close to the water, slow rise.
Light: white foam, blue-grey hull, black smoke.
Sound: machinery, rushing water, continuing guns.
Ends on: Akagi steady inside the gunsmoke.

[AUDIO]
Speak only the quoted lines, once each, in the stated language, at the stated moment. Diegetic sound only — wind, sea, engines, guns, metal, wordless voices — carried across cuts. No music, no narration.

C06 · 편집 위치 03:42–04:02

구축함 옆에 떨어지는 어뢰 · 함교로

20초 · 4샷 · 첨부 7장 · 대사 0

구축함 외경에는 추락하는 비행기 없이 어뢰만 옆으로 떨어진다. 포연을 지나 아카기 함교로 다가가며 나구모가 있는 공간으로 들어간다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C05의 포연과 이어지는 포성을 잇는다.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
창틀이 화면을 덮었다 열리면 함교 내부. 나구모의 등.

첫 화면
구축함 외경. 두 개의 굴뚝, 포좌의 포연, 선체가 화면 오른쪽 절반을 채운다. 하늘에서 어뢰 하나가 떨어진다.

샷별 연출 4개 · 초 단위 동작
샷 010–5초 · 5초 · SPLASH — beside the destroyer

구도 수면 높이. 화면 오른쪽 절반: 구축함(@image2) 선체와 두 굴뚝. 왼쪽 위 하늘: 떨어지는 어뢰(@image3), 비행기는 프레임 밖.

  1. 0–1.5초 어뢰가 기수를 살짝 숙이고 하늘에서 떨어진다.
  2. 1.5–3초 선체 옆 20m에 얕게 입수하며 좁은 흰 물기둥이 솟는다.
  3. 3–5초 물보라가 낮은 난간을 덮고 승무원들이 몸을 피한다. 선체는 맞지 않는다.

카메라 수면 위 낮은 고정, 물기둥에서 짧은 진동.

빛 하얀 물기둥, 회색 선체.

소리 떨어지는 어뢰의 바람 소리, 입수음, 물보라.

구축함 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 물보라가 가라앉는 난간.

참고 @image1 · @image2 · @image3

샷 025–10초 · 5초 · RUNNING — and missing

구도 구축함 난간 위. 전경: 난간과 손가락으로 가리키는 승무원. 아래 해면: 선체와 나란히 달리는 기포 항적.

  1. 5–7초 승무원들이 난간에 붙어 아래 항적을 가리키며 외친다(단어 없음).
  2. 7–9초 구축함이 키를 틀어 선수가 돌고 항적이 선체에서 멀어진다.
  3. 9–10초 항적이 빈 바다로 사라진다.

카메라 35mm 핸드헬드, 난간 위에서 아래로.

빛 물보라 젖은 갑판의 반사.

소리 고함, 기관, 물살.

구축함 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 빈 바다를 보는 승무원들.

샷 0310–14초 · 4초 · FIRE ON — the destroyer's guns

구도 구축함 포좌 미디엄. 사수·급탄수, 뒤로 연기 낀 하늘과 멀리 아카기.

  1. 10–12초 사수가 하늘로 포를 돌려 연사한다.
  2. 12–14초 급탄수가 탄창을 교체하고 탄피가 갑판을 구른다.

카메라 40mm, 낮은 앵글, 반동 진동.

빛 섬광, 연기, 역광.

소리 연사, 탄피.

샷 0414–20초 · 6초 · APPROACH — the bridge

구도 아카기 섬 외경, 함교 창 높이. 전경: 섬 옆 포좌의 포신(흐림). 중경: 함교 창들과 그 안의 실루엣들. 배경: 대공 연기, 가로지르는 미군기.

  1. 14–16초 카메라가 포좌 옆을 지나 함교 창 쪽으로 다가간다. 포좌가 사격 중.
  2. 16–18초 창 너머 군모를 쓴 나구모의 실루엣이 보인다. 미군기가 창 앞 하늘을 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 지난다.
  3. 18–20초 창틀 기둥이 화면을 완전히 덮는다(이 순간 컷).

카메라 섬 바깥의 크레인형 이동, 창틀에서 와이프 컷.

빛 함교 유리의 반사, 안쪽 어둠.

소리 포좌 사격, 바람, 유리 떨림.

카메라 이동 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 화면을 덮은 창틀 기둥.

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • 일본 대공포 사수 — 21세. 마른 얼굴, 날카로운 턱, 철모 아래 삭발, 악문 이, 흰 러닝셔츠. 연장 기관포 조준석.
  • 일본 대공포 급탄수 — 19세. 둥근 앳된 얼굴, 짧은 삭발, 넓은 미간, 상기된 볼, 목의 흰 수건. 15발 상자 탄창 장전.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

구축함 외경에 추락기 없음(대본). 어뢰는 선체 옆에 입수 후 빗나감. 함교 진입은 창틀 가림 컷, 카메라가 유리를 통과하지 않는다.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (6,722자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Late morning: high sun, gunsmoke haze drifting across the fleet.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] Kagero-class destroyer: hull, two funnels, gun mounts
@image3 [ASSET] US Mark 13 aerial torpedo: long body, nose, tail, mount
@image4 [ASSET] carrier Akagi: hull, hinomaru on bow, island on PORT side, downward-curved funnel on STARBOARD side
@image5 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image6 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear
@image7 [BG] Akagi bridge interior: narrow windows, chart table, pillars, passage

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
JAPANESE AA GUNNER — Japanese sailor, 21. Thin face, sharp jaw, shaved head under a steel helmet with chin strap, gritted teeth, white undershirt, sweat; sits in the pointer seat of a twin 25mm mount.
JAPANESE AA LOADER — Japanese sailor, 19. Round boyish face, near-shaved head, wide-set eyes, flushed cheeks, white towel around his neck, slams 15-round box magazines onto the gun.
Americans: varied European-American ancestry (Irish, Italian, German, Polish), varied hair colour, deeper-set eyes, higher nose bridges, forearm hair, sunburnt red-pink skin, stubble, sweat. Real 1942 faces — no movie-star polish.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
NO aircraft crash in the destroyer shots — only the torpedo. The camera never passes through glass.

[STRUCTURE]
20s, 4 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: a destroyer's exterior: two funnels, gunsmoke over the mounts, hull filling the right half; a torpedo falls from the sky.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–5s) — SPLASH — beside the destroyer
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image3
Frame: Water level. Right half: the destroyer (@image2) — hull and two funnels. Upper left sky: a falling torpedo (@image3); its aircraft already out of frame.
Action:
- 0–1.5s: a torpedo falls from the sky, nose slightly down
- 1.5–3s: it enters the water 20 metres off the hull; a narrow white column rises
- 3–5s: spray sheets over the low rail; crew duck; the hull is untouched
Camera: low fixed shot above the water, a short shake at the splash.
Light: white column, grey hull.
Sound: the torpedo's falling whistle, the entry slap, spray.
Ends on: spray settling on the rail.

Shot 2 (5–10s) · CUT TO — RUNNING — and missing
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image3, @image6
Frame: On the destroyer's rail. Foreground: the rail and a crewman pointing. Below: a bubble wake running alongside the hull.
Action:
- 5–7s: crewmen at the rail point down at the wake, wordless shouts
- 7–9s: the destroyer swings her bow; the wake slides away from the hull
- 9–10s: the wake vanishes into open sea
Camera: 35mm handheld, from the rail looking down.
Light: glare off the wet deck.
Sound: shouts, machinery, rushing water.
Ends on: crew staring at empty sea.

Shot 3 (10–14s) · CUT TO — FIRE ON — the destroyer's guns
Refs: @image5, @image6, @image2
Frame: Medium on the destroyer's AA mount: AA GUNNER and AA LOADER; smoky sky and distant Akagi behind.
Action:
- 10–12s: the gunner swings skyward and fires
- 12–14s: the loader swaps magazines; brass rolls across the deck
Camera: 40mm low angle, recoil shake.
Light: muzzle flash, smoke, backlight.
Sound: bursts, brass.
Ends on: gunsmoke drifting toward Akagi's island.

Shot 4 (14–20s) · CUT TO — APPROACH — the bridge
Refs: @image4, @image5, @image7
Frame: Akagi's island at bridge-window height. Foreground: barrels of an island AA tub, soft. Midground: the bridge windows with silhouettes inside. Background: flak and crossing American aircraft.
Action:
- 14–16s: the camera moves past the firing tub toward the bridge windows
- 16–18s: through the glass, Nagumo's capped silhouette; an American aircraft crosses the sky right-to-left in front
- 18–20s: a window pillar fills the frame completely — cut on this wipe
Camera: crane-like move outside the island, wipe cut on the pillar.
Light: reflections on the bridge glass, dark interior.
Sound: tub fire, wind, rattling glass.
Ends on: the window pillar filling the frame.

[AUDIO]
Speak only the quoted lines, once each, in the stated language, at the stated moment. Diegetic sound only — wind, sea, engines, guns, metal, wordless voices — carried across cuts. No music, no narration.

C07 · 편집 위치 04:02–04:30

아수라장 속 첫 명령

28초 · 6샷 · 첨부 9장 · 대사 2

함교 밖은 아수라장이다. 미군기가 창 앞을 가로지르고 함대의 포가 쉬지 않는다. 그 속에서 나구모는 미드웨이 2차 공격을 위한 육상 폭탄 교체를 명령한다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C06의 창틀 와이프에서 이어진다.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
명령이 전달되고, 나구모는 창밖 불타는 미군기를 본다. 외부 포성이 다음 클립의 미 함대 부감 엔진음으로 넘어간다.

첫 화면
함교 내부, 창틀 기둥이 오른쪽으로 빠지며 열린다. 나구모의 등 너머 창 가득 대공 연기와 가로지르는 미군기.

샷 010–5초 · 5초 · CHAOS — through the glass

구도 함교 와이드. 전경: 해도대 모서리와 흔들리는 전성관. 중경: 해도대를 짚은 나구모(등, 군모), 오가는 장교들. 뒤 벽 전체가 창: B-26이 창 앞을 낮게 가로지르고, 대공 연기, 구축함의 포구 섬광, 근탄 물기둥.

  1. 0–2초 B-26이 창 앞을 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 낮게 지나가며 창 전체가 떨린다.
  2. 2–3.5초 배가 기울며 장교 한 명이 비틀거리고 해도 위 컴퍼스가 미끄러진다.
  3. 3.5–5초 근탄 물기둥이 창을 덮었다 쏟아져 내린다. 나구모는 해도대를 짚은 채 움직이지 않는다.

카메라 24mm 와이드, 실내 깊은 곳에서 느린 푸시 인.

빛 창의 강한 백라이트, 실내의 포연 헤이즈, 바깥 섬광이 실내를 깜빡인다.

소리 막힌 포성, 유리 떨림, 강철의 신음, 전성관 목소리(단어 없음).

일본 인물 좌→우 / 미군기 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 창을 보는 나구모의 등.

샷 025–9초 · 4초 · NAGUMO — he watches

구도 나구모 얼굴 클로즈업, 화면 왼쪽 3분의 1에서 오른쪽(창)을 본다. 군모 차양 그늘. 배경: 흐린 창과 섬광.

  1. 5–7초 창밖 폭발 섬광이 얼굴을 주황으로 한 번 칠한다. 그는 눈 하나 깜빡이지 않는다.
  2. 7–9초 콧수염 아래 입술이 굳게 다물리고 턱 근육이 움직인다.

연기 겉으로는 침착, 눈가에만 압박.

샷 039–15초 · 6초 · ORDER — land bombs

  1. 9–10.5초 나구모가 창에서 몸을 돌려 참모를 본다.
  2. 10.5–13.5초 단호하게 명령한다.
  3. 13.5–15초 참모가 받아 적으며 고개를 끄덕인다. 창 뒤로 대공 연기가 또 핀다.

나구모 · 10.5–13.5초 · ミッドウェーへ第二次攻撃準備！陸用爆弾に換装せよ！ (미드웨이로 2차 공격 준비! 육상 폭탄으로 교체하라!)

샷 0518–23초 · 5초 · RELAY — the order goes out

구도 통신 위치. 전경: 무전기 다이얼(@image9). 중경: 수화기를 든 통신병과 쪽지를 건네는 참모. 배경: 작은 창, 바깥 물보라.

  1. 18–20초 참모가 쪽지를 건네고 통신병이 수화기에 대고 빠르게 전달한다(입 모양만, 대사 없음).

  3. 21.5–23초 물이 흘러내리며 빛이 돌아오고, 통신병이 다이얼을 돌린다.

샷 0623–28초 · 5초 · BURNING — out the window

구도 나구모 어깨 너머로 창. 창 너머 멀리: 불붙은 미군기가 연기를 끌며 바다로 떨어진다. 함대의 포연.

  1. 23–25.5초 불붙은 기체가 연기 줄을 끌며 바다에 떨어진다.
  2. 25.5–27초 나구모가 짧게 숨을 내쉰다.
  3. 27–28초 흰 장갑 낀 주먹이 해도 위에 놓인다.

카메라 65mm 어깨 너머, 락 오프.

빛 창빛 실루엣, 먼 불길.

소리 멀리 낙수음, 이어지는 포성.

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • 나구모 — 55세. 승인본 에셋 최우선. 넓고 무거운 얼굴, 깊은 팔자주름, 처진 눈, 짧은 회색 칫솔 콧수염. 모든 샷 군모, 흰 장갑.
  • 일본 함교 참모 (대본의 부하2) — 42세. 갸름한 계란형, 좁은 턱, 가는 금속테 사각 안경, 면도, 굳게 다문 입, 전문 용지 클립보드.
  • 일본 통신병 — 22세. 마른 앳된 얼굴, 둥근 볼, 삭발, 천 모자 위 헤드셋, 귀에 꽂은 연필.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

나구모 군모 항상. 창밖 전투가 모든 샷에서 보이거나 들린다(창·진동·막힌 포성). 대본의 '구축함 선실'은 아카기 함교로 각색.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (8,418자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Late morning, interior: hard white window light, gunsmoke haze inside the bridge, flashes from outside.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] Akagi bridge interior: narrow windows, chart table, pillars, passage
@image2 [ASSET] Nagumo approved face, age, mustache, dark uniform, peaked cap
@image3 [ASSET] Japanese staff officer uniform and cap
@image4 [ASSET] carrier Akagi: hull, hinomaru on bow, island on PORT side, downward-curved funnel on STARBOARD side
@image5 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image6 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear
@image8 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight
@image9 [ASSET] Japanese fixed radio set, dials, corded handset

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
NAGUMO — Japanese vice admiral, 55 (approved reference image governs). Broad heavy face, deep nasolabial folds, heavy-lidded drooping eyes, short grey toothbrush mustache, grey close-cropped sideburns. Peaked naval cap in EVERY shot. Dark navy uniform, white gloves.
STAFF OFFICER — Japanese officer, 42. Slim oval face, narrow chin, thin metal-rimmed rectangular glasses, clean-shaven, neat hair under his cap, composed but tight mouth, clipboard of message forms.
SIGNALMAN — Japanese sailor, 22. Thin boyish face, round cheeks, near-shaved head, headset clamped over a cloth cap, pencil behind his ear.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
Outside the windows is NEVER calm: in every shot there are aircraft, flak, splashes or gunfire visible, and the glass rattles. Nagumo always wears his cap.

[STRUCTURE]
28s, 6 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: Akagi bridge interior as a window pillar slides off to the right: beyond Nagumo's back, the windows are full of flak and a crossing American aircraft.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–5s) — CHAOS — through the glass
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image4, @image8, @image6
Frame: Bridge wide. Foreground: the chart-table corner and a swinging voice tube. Midground: NAGUMO from behind gripping the chart table, cap on; officers moving. The back wall is windows: a B-26 crosses low, flak bursts, a destroyer's muzzle flashes, near-miss columns.
Action:
- 0–2s: a B-26 passes low right-to-left; every pane rattles
- 2–3.5s: the ship heels; an officer staggers; dividers slide across the chart
- 3.5–5s: a near-miss column sheets over the glass and falls away; Nagumo, hands on the table, does not move
Camera: 24mm wide from deep inside, slow push-in.
Light: hard window backlight, gunsmoke haze inside, outside flashes strobing the room.
Sound: muffled gunfire, rattling glass, groaning steel, wordless voice-tube chatter.
Ends on: Nagumo's back facing the windows.

Shot 2 (5–9s) · CUT TO — NAGUMO — he watches
Refs: @image2, @image1
Frame: Close-up on NAGUMO, left third, looking right toward the windows; cap-peak shadow; soft windows and flashes behind.
Action:
- 5–7s: an outside explosion washes his face orange once; he does not blink
- 7–9s: his lips press tight under the mustache; a jaw muscle works
Acting: composed on the surface; pressure only around the eyes.
Camera: 85mm, locked-off, only ship vibration.
Light: cold window light plus one orange flash.
Sound: a blast, rattling glass.
Ends on: his eyes setting on a decision.

Shot 3 (9–15s) · CUT TO — ORDER — land bombs
Refs: @image2, @image3, @image1
Frame: Two-shot: NAGUMO left, STAFF OFFICER right with his clipboard; between them, the window — a firing AA tub, an American aircraft.
Action:
- 9–10.5s: Nagumo turns from the window to the staff officer
- 10.5–13.5s: he gives the order, firm
- 13.5–15s: the officer writes and nods; another flak burst blooms behind the glass
Dialogue (NAGUMO, Japanese, firm, clipped) at 10.5–13.5s: "ミッドウェーへ第二次攻撃準備！陸用爆弾に換装せよ！"
Camera: 50mm, very slow dolly-in between them.
Light: window backlight, side light on faces.
Sound: the order, muffled guns, pencil scratching.
Ends on: the officer writing.

Shot 4 (15–18s) · CUT TO — ACK
Refs: @image3, @image1
Frame: Close-up on the STAFF OFFICER, right third; window light in his glasses.
Action:
- 15–16.5s: he answers, short
- 16.5–18s: he turns at once toward the signal station
Dialogue (STAFF OFFICER, Japanese, crisp) at 15–16.5s: "はっ！"
Camera: 65mm, short pan as he turns.
Light: reflection in his lenses.
Sound: the reply, shoes on steel.
Ends on: the officer heading to the radio.

Shot 5 (18–23s) · CUT TO — RELAY — the order goes out
Refs: @image9, @image3, @image1, @image5
Frame: Signal station. Foreground: radio dials (@image9). Midground: the SIGNALMAN with the handset, the STAFF OFFICER handing him a slip. Background: a small window, spray outside.
Action:
- 18–20s: the officer hands over the slip; the signalman relays it rapidly into the handset — mouth moving, no audible line
- 20–21.5s: near-miss spray covers the small window; the room darkens for a moment
- 21.5–23s: water streams off, light returns; the signalman turns a dial
Camera: 50mm, low beside the radio.
Light: small dial lamps, spray shadows.
Sound: radio static, rapid murmuring, water hitting glass.
Ends on: a hand turning the dial.

Shot 6 (23–28s) · CUT TO — BURNING — out the window
Refs: @image2, @image1, @image5, @image8
Frame: Over NAGUMO's shoulder through the window: far off, a burning American aircraft trails smoke into the sea; gunsmoke over the fleet.
Action:
- 23–25.5s: the burning aircraft falls, trailing smoke into the sea
- 25.5–27s: Nagumo exhales, short
- 27–28s: his white-gloved fist settles on the chart
Camera: 65mm over the shoulder, locked-off.

C08 · 편집 위치 04:30–04:58

미 함대 부감 · 열 척의 보고

28초 · 6샷 · 첨부 9장 · 대사 3

미국 함대가 실재한다는 것을 먼저 보여준 뒤, 함교로 돌아와 참모가 달려와 군함 열 척 발견을 보고한다. 나구모는 과장되게 당황하고, 창밖은 여전히 시끄럽다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C07의 외부 포성에서 넘어온다(미국 쪽 고요한 바다로 대비).

다음으로 넘기는 상황
창이 물보라로 덮였다 열리며 고민하는 나구모의 반사. 다음 클립 미 항모 출격으로 컷.

첫 화면
높은 부감. 엔터프라이즈와 호위함들이 긴 항적을 그리며 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 항진한다. 구름 조각이 전경을 스친다.

샷 010–6초 · 6초 · THE US FLEET — it is real

구도 높은 사선 부감. 가운데: 엔터프라이즈(@image2, 직선 비행갑판, 우현 섬). 주변: 순양함·구축함 호위, 평행한 흰 항적들. 전경: 구름 조각.

  1. 0–2초 구름 조각이 걷히며 함대가 드러난다.
  2. 2–4초 엔터프라이즈 갑판 위에 줄지어 선 기체들이 보인다.
  3. 4–6초 호위함들이 열을 지어 같은 방향으로 따라온다.

카메라 높은 부감, 함대를 따라 오른쪽→왼쪽 느린 공중 추적.

빛 눈부신 바다, 하얀 항적, 고요.

소리 고공의 바람만. 전투음 없음.

미 함대 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 화면을 가로지르는 함대.

샷 026–10초 · 4초 · THE MESSAGE

구도 함교 통신 위치. 통신병이 전문 용지를 찢어 낸다. 참모가 낚아채 달린다. 뒤 창: 대공 연기.

  1. 6–7.5초 통신병이 연필을 던지듯 놓고 용지를 찢는다.
  2. 7.5–10초 참모가 용지를 들고 함교를 가로질러 달린다. 오가는 장교들 사이를 몸을 틀어 빠져나간다.

카메라 35mm 핸드헬드, 참모를 따라 달림.

빛 창의 백라이트, 섬광.

소리 종이 찢는 소리, 발소리, 막힌 포성.

참모 이동 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 나구모 앞에 멈추는 참모.

참고 @image6 · @image5 · @image3

샷 0310–14초 · 4초 · THE REPORT

구도 오버 숄더. 나구모 어깨 너머 참모(오른쪽). 숨이 차다.

  1. 10–11초 참모가 용지를 내민다.
  2. 11–14초 숨을 고르며 보고한다.

일본 참모 (부하2) · 11–14초 · 敵艦十隻、発見との報告です！ (적의 군함 열 척이 발견됐다는 보고입니다!)

카메라 50mm 오버 숄더.

빛 창빛, 안경 반사.

소리 숨, 보고, 유리 떨림.

참모 얼굴 우→좌(나구모를 향해).

마지막 프레임 용지를 잡는 나구모의 흰 장갑.

샷 0414–19초 · 5초 · WHAT? — flustered

구도 나구모 미디엄 클로즈업, 화면 왼쪽. 뒤 창: 미군기 통과와 포좌 사격.

  1. 14–15.5초 눈이 용지에서 창으로, 다시 용지로 튄다.
  2. 15.5–18초 목소리가 높아지며 과장되게 당황한다.
  3. 18–19초 창밖에 폭발이 번쩍여 그의 몸이 먼저 움찔한다.

연기 이전 영상과 다르게: 침착한 장군이 아니라 처음으로 흔들리는 사람. 과장된 당황 → 말이 끊긴다.

나구모 · 15.5–18초 · 何だと？……いきなり、どこから現れた？ (뭐라고? ……갑자기 어디서 나타난 거야?)

카메라 65mm, 그의 동요에 맞춘 짧은 핸드헬드 흔들림.

빛 창의 섬광이 얼굴을 친다.

소리 높아진 일본어, 폭발.

나구모 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 벌어진 입, 흔들리는 눈.

샷 0519–24초 · 5초 · CONFLICT — two orders

구도 해도대 위 하이 앵글. 미드웨이 공격 명령서와 새 보고 용지가 나란히. 나구모의 두 손.

  1. 19–20.5초 나구모가 흰 장갑 한쪽을 벗어 해도 위에 던진다.
  2. 20.5–22.5초 맨손으로 콧수염을 쓸며 두 장을 번갈아 본다. 한숨 섞인 혼잣말.
  3. 22.5–24초 손이 한쪽 명령서를 집으려다 허공에서 멈춘다.

나구모 · 20.5–22.5초 · はあ……これは一体…… (하…… 이게 대체……)

카메라 하이 앵글에서 그의 얼굴까지 천천히 틸트 업.

빛 해도 위 창빛, 깜빡이는 섬광.

소리 혼잣말, 진동하는 해도대, 막힌 포성.

나구모 좌→우.

마지막 프레임 허공에서 멈춘 손.

샷 0624–28초 · 4초 · REFLECTION — no quiet

구도 창 바깥쪽에서 본 창. 유리에 나구모의 흐린 반사, 그 위로 바깥 포좌와 바다가 겹친다.

  1. 24–26초 근탄 물기둥이 창을 덮는다.
  2. 26–28초 물이 흘러내리며 안쪽에서 미동 없이 고민하는 나구모가 다시 보인다. 포좌는 계속 쏜다.

카메라 창 바깥 고정. 카메라는 유리를 통과하지 않는다.

빛 유리의 반사와 투과가 겹친다.

소리 물이 유리를 때리는 소리, 연사.

나구모 정면에 가까운 사선.

마지막 프레임 유리 너머의 나구모.

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • 나구모 — 55세. 승인본 에셋 최우선. 넓고 무거운 얼굴, 깊은 팔자주름, 처진 눈, 짧은 회색 칫솔 콧수염. 모든 샷 군모, 흰 장갑.
  • 일본 함교 참모 (대본의 부하2) — 42세. 갸름한 계란형, 좁은 턱, 가는 금속테 사각 안경, 면도, 굳게 다문 입, 전문 용지 클립보드.
  • 일본 통신병 — 22세. 마른 앳된 얼굴, 둥근 볼, 삭발, 천 모자 위 헤드셋, 귀에 꽂은 연필.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

열 척 = 군함 열 척(항모 열 척 아님). 엔터프라이즈 섬은 우현. 나구모 군모 항상. 창밖 전투 지속.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (8,164자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Late morning. US fleet: calm bright sea, clean white wakes. Akagi bridge: hard window light, gunsmoke haze, flashes outside.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] USS Enterprise CV-6 1942: straight flight deck, STARBOARD island, hull
@image3 [BG] Akagi bridge interior: narrow windows, chart table, pillars, passage
@image4 [ASSET] Nagumo approved face, age, mustache, dark uniform, peaked cap
@image5 [ASSET] Japanese staff officer uniform and cap
@image6 [ASSET] Japanese fixed radio set, dials, corded handset
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image8 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear
@image9 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
NAGUMO — Japanese vice admiral, 55 (approved reference image governs). Broad heavy face, deep nasolabial folds, heavy-lidded drooping eyes, short grey toothbrush mustache, grey close-cropped sideburns. Peaked naval cap in EVERY shot. Dark navy uniform, white gloves.
STAFF OFFICER — Japanese officer, 42. Slim oval face, narrow chin, thin metal-rimmed rectangular glasses, clean-shaven, neat hair under his cap, composed but tight mouth, clipboard of message forms.
SIGNALMAN — Japanese sailor, 22. Thin boyish face, round cheeks, near-shaved head, headset clamped over a cloth cap, pencil behind his ear.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
The report says ten warships — never show or say ten carriers. Outside Akagi's windows is never calm. Nagumo always wears his cap.

[STRUCTURE]
28s, 6 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: high aerial: Enterprise and her escorts steam right-to-left drawing long wakes; a cloud wisp crosses the foreground.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–6s) — THE US FLEET — it is real
Refs: @image1, @image2
Frame: High oblique aerial. Centre: Enterprise (@image2) — straight flight deck, island to STARBOARD. Around her: cruiser and destroyer escorts, parallel white wakes. Foreground: a cloud wisp.
Action:
- 0–2s: a cloud wisp clears, revealing the fleet
- 2–4s: aircraft ranged on Enterprise's deck come into view
- 4–6s: escorts follow in column, same heading
Camera: high aerial, slow tracking right-to-left with the fleet.
Light: brilliant sea, white wakes, calm.
Sound: only high wind — no battle noise.
Ends on: the fleet spread across the frame.

Shot 2 (6–10s) · CUT TO — THE MESSAGE
Refs: @image6, @image5, @image3
Frame: Bridge signal station: the SIGNALMAN tears a message form off the pad; the STAFF OFFICER snatches it and runs; flak beyond the windows.
Action:
- 6–7.5s: the signalman drops his pencil and tears the form off
- 7.5–10s: the officer runs across the bridge with it, twisting between officers
Camera: 35mm handheld, running with the officer.
Light: window backlight, flashes.
Sound: paper tearing, footsteps, muffled guns.
Ends on: the officer stopping before Nagumo.

Shot 3 (10–14s) · CUT TO — THE REPORT
Refs: @image5, @image4, @image3
Frame: Over NAGUMO's shoulder onto the STAFF OFFICER, right, breathless.
Action:
- 10–11s: he holds out the form
- 11–14s: he reports, catching his breath
Dialogue (STAFF OFFICER, Japanese, urgent, breathless) at 11–14s: "敵艦十隻、発見との報告です！"
Camera: 50mm over the shoulder.
Light: window light, lens glare.
Sound: breath, the report, rattling glass.
Ends on: Nagumo's white glove taking the form.

Shot 4 (14–19s) · CUT TO — WHAT? — flustered
Refs: @image4, @image3, @image9, @image7
Frame: NAGUMO medium close-up, frame left; behind, through the window, an American aircraft passing and a tub firing.
Action:
- 14–15.5s: his eyes jump from the paper to the window and back
- 15.5–18s: his voice rises — exaggerated alarm
- 18–19s: a blast flashes outside; his body flinches first
Acting: unlike before: not the calm admiral but a man shaken for the first time — exaggerated alarm, then words failing.
Dialogue (NAGUMO, Japanese, loud, exaggerated alarm) at 15.5–18s: "何だと？……いきなり、どこから現れた？"
Camera: 65mm, a short handheld wobble matching his agitation.
Light: window flashes hitting his face.
Sound: the raised voice, a blast.
Ends on: mouth open, eyes unsteady.

Shot 5 (19–24s) · CUT TO — CONFLICT — two orders
Refs: @image4, @image3
Frame: High angle over the chart table: the Midway attack order and the new report side by side; NAGUMO's hands.
Action:
- 19–20.5s: Nagumo pulls off one white glove and drops it on the chart
- 20.5–22.5s: he rubs his mustache with the bare hand, eyes moving between the two papers; a half-sighed mutter
- 22.5–24s: his hand reaches for one order and stops in mid-air
Dialogue (NAGUMO, Japanese, under his breath, conflicted) at 20.5–22.5s: "はあ……これは一体……"
Camera: high angle, slow tilt up to his face.
Light: window light on the chart, flickering flashes.
Sound: the mutter, the vibrating table, muffled guns.
Ends on: the hand frozen in mid-air.

Shot 6 (24–28s) · CUT TO — REFLECTION — no quiet
Refs: @image4, @image3, @image7, @image1
Frame: The window from outside: NAGUMO's faint reflection on the glass overlaid with the firing tub and sea outside.
Action:
- 24–26s: a near-miss column drenches the glass
- 26–28s: water streams away revealing Nagumo inside, motionless, thinking; the tub keeps firing
Camera: fixed outside the window; the camera never passes through the glass.
Light: reflection and transmission layered on the glass.
Sound: water hitting glass, bursts of fire.
Ends on: Nagumo behind the glass.

[AUDIO]
Speak only the quoted lines, once each, in the stated language, at the stated moment. Diegetic sound only — wind, sea, engines, guns, metal, wordless voices — carried across cuts. No music, no narration.

C09 · 편집 위치 04:58–05:24

미국 항모에서 떠오르는 공격대

26초 · 5샷 · 첨부 8장 · 대사 0

일본이 망설이는 동안 미국 항모는 실제로 공격대를 띄우고 있다. 출발 항모와 이미 떠난 여러 대, 다음 이륙이 한 화면에 들어오고, 편대는 일본 함대 쪽으로 향한다. (일본 → 미국 → 일본 교차의 '미국')

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C08의 고민하는 나구모에서 하드 컷. 소리도 함교의 막힌 포성에서 탁 트인 항모의 엔진 굉음으로.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
공격대가 수평선 쪽으로 멀어지고 항모는 오른쪽 뒤에 남는다. 다음 컷은 다시 나구모.

첫 화면
엔터프라이즈 선수 앞쪽 바다 위 낮은 위치. 선수 너머로 이미 떠난 두 대가 왼쪽으로 오르고, 세 번째가 갑판 끝을 막 벗어난다.

번호순 첨부 이미지 8장 · 기존 에셋 그대로

모두 참고 이미지로 첨부한다. 시작·종료 프레임 지정 없음. 아래 기존 경로의 파일을 이 번호 순서로 올린다.

샷별 연출 5개 · 초 단위 동작
샷 010–5초 · 5초 · LAUNCH — carrier and aircraft in one frame

구도 수면 위 낮게, 선수 앞쪽 사선. 오른쪽: 엔터프라이즈(@image2) 선수와 비행갑판 가장자리. 왼쪽 하늘: 이미 오른 두 대. 갑판 끝: 막 떠오르는 세 번째. 갑판 뒤: 프로펠러를 돌리는 다음 기체들.

  1. 0–2초 세 번째 기체가 갑판 끝에서 살짝 가라앉았다가 다시 오른다.
  2. 2–3.5초 앞선 두 대가 왼쪽 하늘로 선회하며 고도를 높인다.
  3. 3.5–5초 갑판 위 다음 기체가 출발선으로 굴러온다.

카메라 24mm, 수면 위 2m, 선수 쪽으로 느린 트래킹.

빛 밝은 하늘, 선수 물보라, 깨끗한 바다.

소리 항모를 가르는 파도, 여러 엔진, 갑판 바람.

미국 기체 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 하늘에 셋, 갑판에 다음.

참고 @image1 · @image2 · @image5 · @image6

샷 025–10초 · 5초 · THE FLAG

구도 비행갑판 높이. 전경 오른쪽: 비행갑판 장교(체크 깃발). 중경: 브레이크를 건 채 최대 출력의 F4F(@image4), 꼬리가 들썩인다. 배경: 갑판 가장자리에 웅크린 승무원들, 대기 기체들.

  1. 5–7초 장교가 깃발을 머리 위로 빠르게 돌린다. 엔진 굉음이 최고조.
  2. 7–8초 깃발을 앞으로 내리꽂으며 몸을 낮춘다.
  3. 8–10초 F4F가 브레이크를 풀고 갑판을 오른쪽→왼쪽으로 질주한다. 프로펠러 바람에 장교의 옷이 펄럭인다.

연기 장교는 무표정하게 리듬을 탄다. 수천 번 해 본 동작.

카메라 35mm, 갑판 위 낮게, 출발 후 기체를 따라 팬.

빛 강한 해, 노란 상의, 반짝이는 프로펠러 원판.

소리 최대 출력 엔진, 바람, 갑판 진동.

미국 기체 우→좌.

마지막 프레임 갑판 끝으로 달려가는 F4F.

샷 0310–14초 · 4초 · OFF THE BOW

구도 갑판 끝 옆 캣워크. 전경: 캣워크 난간. 중경: 갑판 끝을 벗어나는 F4F. 배경: 바다.

  1. 10–11.5초 F4F가 갑판 끝을 벗어나며 한 번 가라앉는다.
  2. 11.5–13초 바퀴를 접으며 상승한다.
  3. 13–14초 물보라가 캣워크를 적신다.

카메라 50mm, 캣워크에서 상승을 따라 틸트 업.

빛 역광, 물보라 반짝임.

소리 엔진 도플러, 물보라.

샷 0414–19초 · 5초 · THE HEAVY ONES — torpedo bombers

구도 갑판 뒤쪽에서 본 이륙 활주. TBD(@image6)가 배 아래 어뢰를 단 채 무겁게 굴러간다. 조종석에 조종사와 후방 사수(얼굴 디테일 없음).

  1. 14–16초 TBD가 무겁게 가속하며 꼬리가 뜬다.
  2. 16–18초 갑판 끝에서 겨우 떠오르며 어뢰가 수면 위를 아슬아슬하게 스친다.
  3. 18–19초 그 뒤로 다음 TBD가 출발선에 선다.

카메라 75mm, 갑판 뒤 높은 위치에서 압축.

빛 열기 아지랑이, 강한 해.

샷 0519–26초 · 7초 · STRIKE GROUP — heading for Japan

구도 높은 공중. 전경: 가까운 F4F 날개. 중경: 저공의 TBD 줄과 그 위 F4F 엄호. 오른쪽 뒤 아래: 작아지는 엔터프라이즈와 항적. 왼쪽: 수평선(일본 함대 방향).

  1. 19–21.5초 공격대가 항모 위에서 대형을 짠다.
  2. 21.5–24초 편대가 오른쪽→왼쪽 수평선을 향해 기수를 맞춘다.
  3. 24–26초 가까운 날개가 화면을 가르며 지나가자 더 큰 편대가 드러난다.

카메라 공중 추적, 날개 와이프로 끝.

빛 높은 해, 구름 그림자.

  • 미 항모 비행갑판 장교 — 34세. 마르고 가죽 같은 얼굴, 가늘게 뜬 옅은 눈, 날카로운 코, 노란 상의·천 헬멧, 오른손 체크 깃발.

출발 항모 = 엔터프라이즈 CV-6(기존 에셋). 섬은 우현. 미국 이동 우→좌. 개별 이륙 준비 반복 인서트 없음.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (7,198자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Late morning, US carrier: bright open sky, clean sea, wind over the deck, no smoke.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image2 [ASSET] USS Enterprise CV-6 1942: straight flight deck, STARBOARD island, hull
@image3 [BG] Enterprise wooden flight deck, deck edge, launch lane
@image4 [ASSET] F4F-4 Wildcat on deck, wings spread, wheels down
@image5 [ASSET] F4F-4 Wildcat in flight, main wheels retracted
@image6 [ASSET] TBD-1 Devastator: long tandem canopy, blue-grey, corrugated wings, main wheels half-protruding when retracted
@image7 [ASSET] US flight-deck crew clothing, cloth helmet, signal role
@image8 [ASSET] US Navy aircrew flight suit, helmet, yellow life vest

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
FLIGHT DECK OFFICER — American Navy, 34. Lean leathery face, squinting pale eyes, sharp nose, yellow jersey and cloth helmet, a chequered flag in his right hand.
Americans: varied European-American ancestry (Irish, Italian, German, Polish), varied hair colour, deeper-set eyes, higher nose bridges, forearm hair, sunburnt red-pink skin, stubble, sweat. Real 1942 faces — no movie-star polish.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
Show the carrier and multiple airborne aircraft together — the origin must be obvious. No repeated single-aircraft preparation inserts. Enterprise's island stays on her STARBOARD side.

[STRUCTURE]
26s, 5 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: low over the sea off Enterprise's bow: beyond the bow two aircraft already climb away to the left, a third just leaving the deck edge.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–5s) — LAUNCH — carrier and aircraft in one frame
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image5, @image6
Frame: Low over the water, front three-quarter off the bow. Right: Enterprise's (@image2) bow and deck edge. Left sky: two aircraft already up. Deck edge: a third lifting off. Behind it: more aircraft with props turning.
Action:
- 0–2s: the third aircraft dips off the deck edge, then climbs
- 2–3.5s: the two ahead bank left and climb
- 3.5–5s: the next aircraft rolls up to the launch spot
Camera: 24mm, 2 metres above the water, slow tracking toward the bow.
Light: bright sky, bow spray, clean sea.
Sound: bow wave, many engines, deck wind.
Ends on: three in the sky, the next on deck.

Shot 2 (5–10s) · CUT TO — THE FLAG
Refs: @image3, @image4, @image7
Frame: Deck level. Foreground right: the FLIGHT DECK OFFICER with a chequered flag. Midground: an F4F (@image4) at full power, brakes on, tail bucking. Background: crew crouched at the deck edge, waiting aircraft.
Action:
- 5–7s: the officer whirls the flag fast over his head; the engine screams to full power
- 7–8s: he snaps the flag down and forward, crouching
- 8–10s: the F4F releases and races down the deck right-to-left; propwash whips the officer's jersey
Acting: the officer is expressionless, in rhythm — he has done this a thousand times.
Camera: 35mm low on the deck, pan with the aircraft after release.
Light: hard sun, yellow jersey, glinting propeller disc.
Sound: full-power engine, wind, deck vibration.
Ends on: the F4F racing for the deck end.

Shot 3 (10–14s) · CUT TO — OFF THE BOW
Refs: @image4, @image5, @image1

Action:

- 11.5–13s: it retracts its wheels and climbs
- 13–14s: spray wets the catwalk
Camera: 50mm from the catwalk, tilt up with the climb.
Light: backlight, sparkling spray.
Sound: engine Doppler, spray.
Ends on: the climbing F4F in silhouette.

Shot 4 (14–19s) · CUT TO — THE HEAVY ONES — torpedo bombers
Refs: @image3, @image6, @image8

Action:

- 16–18s: it barely lifts at the deck end, the torpedo just clearing the waves
- 18–19s: behind it the next TBD takes the launch spot

Light: heat shimmer, hard sun.
Sound: heavy engine, deck wind.
Ends on: the TBD just above the sea.

Sound: a chorus of engines, wind.

C10 · 편집 위치 05:24–05:54

안절부절 · 그리고 결단

30초 · 6샷 · 첨부 9장 · 대사 3

다시 아카기 함교. 밖은 여전히 시끄럽다. 안절부절하던 나구모가 결단하고 어뢰·철갑폭탄 재무장을 명령한다. 참모의 우려에 그는 창밖 전투기를 가리키며 밀어붙인다.

프롬프트는 영어(시댄스용) · 샷 카드는 한국어(검토용) · 둘은 같은 내용

앞에서 받는 상황
C09의 미국 공격대 엔진음이 함교의 막힌 포성으로 바뀌며 컷.

다음으로 넘기는 상황
참모의 어깨가 화면을 덮으며 명령이 실행으로 넘어간다. 컷 투 격납고.

첫 화면
함교. 나구모가 창과 해도대 사이를 두 걸음 오가며 흰 장갑을 쥐었다 편다. 창밖에 대공 연기와 미군기.

샷별 연출 6개 · 초 단위 동작
샷 010–5초 · 5초 · RESTLESS

구도 함교 미디엄 와이드. 나구모가 창과 해도대 사이를 오간다. 왼쪽 뒤에 참모, 오른쪽에 통신병. 창: 미군기 통과, 대공 연기.

  1. 0–2초 나구모가 창 쪽으로 두 걸음, 멈추고, 다시 돌아선다.
  2. 2–3.5초 흰 장갑 낀 손을 쥐었다 편다.
  3. 3.5–5초 창밖 미군기가 낮게 지나가자 모두의 시선이 창으로 쏠린다.

연기 결정하지 못한 사람의 몸. 눈은 계속 두 장의 종이로 돌아간다.

카메라 35mm, 그의 움직임을 따라 짧은 팬 왕복.

빛 창의 백라이트, 깜빡이는 섬광.

소리 구두 소리, 막힌 포성, 엔진 통과.

나구모 좌→우 중심.

마지막 프레임 창을 보는 사람들.

  1. 5–6.5초 근탄 폭발로 함교가 덜컹이고 천장에서 먼지가 떨어진다.
  2. 6.5–8초 나구모가 해도대를 움켜쥐고 버틴다.
  3. 8–9초 먼지가 종이 위에 내려앉는다. 그의 눈이 새 보고 쪽에 멈춘다.

카메라 50mm, 하이 앵글에서 얼굴로 틸트 업.

  1. 9–10.5초 미드웨이 공격 명령서를 손등으로 밀어낸다.
  2. 10.5–11.5초 새 보고 용지를 손바닥으로 탁 내리친다.
  3. 11.5–16초 결단한 목소리로 명령한다.

카메라 65mm, 느린 푸시 인.

  1. 16–17.5초 받아 적던 연필이 멈춘다.
  2. 17.5–20초 조심스럽게 우려를 말한다.

  1. 21–22.5초 나구모가 팔을 뻗어 창밖을 가리킨다.
  2. 22.5–25초 강하게 내뱉는다. 창 너머로 제로센 두 대가 지나간다.

연기 목소리는 강하지만 턱의 긴장은 풀리지 않는다.

구도 참모가 통신 위치로 걷고, 나구모는 해도대에 남는다. 지나가는 참모의 어깨가 카메라 앞을 덮는다.

  1. 25–27초 참모가 짧게 고개 숙이고 통신 위치로 향한다.
  2. 27–28.5초 통신병이 수화기를 들고 명령을 받아 적는다.
  3. 28.5–30초 참모의 어깨가 카메라 앞을 완전히 덮는다(컷).

카메라 35mm, 참모를 따라가다 어깨 와이프.

이 클립의 얼굴 고정 (인물 바이블 발췌)
  • 나구모 — 55세. 승인본 에셋 최우선. 넓고 무거운 얼굴, 깊은 팔자주름, 처진 눈, 짧은 회색 칫솔 콧수염. 모든 샷 군모, 흰 장갑.
  • 일본 함교 참모 (대본의 부하2) — 42세. 갸름한 계란형, 좁은 턱, 가는 금속테 사각 안경, 면도, 굳게 다문 입, 전문 용지 클립보드.
  • 일본 통신병 — 22세. 마른 앳된 얼굴, 둥근 볼, 삭발, 천 모자 위 헤드셋, 귀에 꽂은 연필.
동일 인물·무장·손상·공간 상태

나구모 군모 항상. 창밖 전투 지속. 명령 = 어뢰와 철갑폭탄. 같은 명령의 반복 보고 장면 없음.

전체 생성 프롬프트 펼치기 (7,997자)
[STYLE LOCK]
Photoreal live-action WWII epic, Battle of Midway, June 4 1942. Large-format cinema camera, spherical primes, fine film grain, soft highlight roll-off, deep atmospheric haze at distance.
Palette: steel-teal sea, milky high-key sky with towering cumulus, white coral sand, olive-drab, khaki and dark navy, warm tanned skin. Orange fire only at the instant of an explosion or a burning wing; flak is black, smoke charcoal, sea spray white. No oversaturated cyan, no game sheen.
Every frame in three layers: dark foreground occluder (sandbag, barrel, window frame, wing edge, hull rail) — subject — vast sky, sea or battle beyond. Exteriors: men small against the sky. Interiors: hard window light, haze, and the battle outside always visible or audible.
Real-time speed, no slow motion. 16:9, 1080p. Light, haze, smoke and wind stay continuous across cuts.

[TIME & PLACE]
Late morning, interior: hard window light, gunsmoke haze, flashes outside.

[IMAGES — all references, none as start/end frame]
@image1 [BG] Akagi bridge interior: narrow windows, chart table, pillars, passage
@image2 [ASSET] Nagumo approved face, age, mustache, dark uniform, peaked cap
@image3 [ASSET] Japanese staff officer uniform and cap
@image4 [ASSET] Japanese fixed radio set, dials, corded handset
@image5 [BG] open Pacific surface, far horizon, broken cumulus
@image6 [ASSET] Japanese twin 25mm AA mount: two parallel barrels, carriage, sights
@image7 [ASSET] Japanese AA crew uniform, helmet, magazine gear
@image8 [ASSET] early B-26 Marauder twin-engine bomber, olive drab, short wings, wheels retracted in flight
@image9 [ASSET] A6M2 Zero, grey-green, red hinomaru, wheels retracted, no drop tank

[REFERENCE LOCK]
BG images: copy layout, materials and geography only — not their camera, light or colour.
Aircraft/ship/weapon images: exact silhouette, structure, paint, markings, number of engines, barrels and funnels; never morph types.
Costume sheets: uniform and gear only, worn by living men with the CAST LOCK faces. Never show sheets, labels, mannequins or multiple views.
US insignia 1942: white star in blue circle, no red centre, no side bars. Japanese: red hinomaru.

[CAST LOCK — same faces in every shot]
NAGUMO — Japanese vice admiral, 55 (approved reference image governs). Broad heavy face, deep nasolabial folds, heavy-lidded drooping eyes, short grey toothbrush mustache, grey close-cropped sideburns. Peaked naval cap in EVERY shot. Dark navy uniform, white gloves.
STAFF OFFICER — Japanese officer, 42. Slim oval face, narrow chin, thin metal-rimmed rectangular glasses, clean-shaven, neat hair under his cap, composed but tight mouth, clipboard of message forms.
SIGNALMAN — Japanese sailor, 22. Thin boyish face, round cheeks, near-shaved head, headset clamped over a cloth cap, pencil behind his ear.
Japanese: authentic adult Japanese men of 1942 — lean, wiry, compact builds; enlisted heads shaved or near-shaved, officers short-cropped; monolid or hooded eyes, straight lower nose bridge, defined cheekbones, bronze tan, clean-shaven unless stated. Natural, dignified — never caricature, never modern idol styling.
Every other extra has a clearly different face; no clones.

[DIRECTION & PHYSICS]
American flight paths, ship headings, muzzles and profiles read RIGHT-TO-LEFT; Japanese read LEFT-TO-RIGHT — by camera placement, never by mirroring.
Propellers are blurred discs; aircraft keep exact shape; torpedoes fall nose-slightly-down, splash, then run straight leaving a bubble wake; bombs keep forward momentum; recoil, casings, spray and debris obey gravity. A destroyed aircraft never reappears. The camera never passes through glass, walls or hulls.

[NEGATIVE]
No on-screen text, subtitles, timestamps, logos, watermarks, letterbox. No music, no narration. No modern objects. No anime/CG/plastic look, no waxy skin, no cloned faces, no extra fingers or limbs.
Outside the windows is NEVER calm. Nagumo always wears his cap. The order is torpedoes and armour-piercing bombs.

[STRUCTURE]
30s, 6 shots, hard cuts. Timings are clip time.
First frame: Akagi bridge: NAGUMO paces two steps between the window and the chart table, clenching and opening his white gloves; flak and an American aircraft outside.

[SHOTS]
Shot 1 (0–5s) — RESTLESS
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image3, @image8
Frame: Bridge medium-wide: NAGUMO pacing between window and table; STAFF OFFICER behind left, SIGNALMAN right; windows: an American aircraft passing, flak.
Action:
- 0–2s: Nagumo takes two steps to the window, stops, turns back
- 2–3.5s: he clenches and opens a white-gloved hand
- 3.5–5s: an American aircraft passes low outside; every head turns to the window
Acting: the body of a man who cannot decide; his eyes keep returning to the two papers.
Camera: 35mm, short pans back and forth with his pacing.
Light: window backlight, flickering flashes.
Sound: shoes on steel, muffled guns, an aircraft passing.
Ends on: all eyes on the window.

Shot 2 (5–9s) · CUT TO — JOLT
Refs: @image1, @image2, @image5

Action:
- 5–6.5s: a near miss jolts the bridge; dust sifts from the ceiling
- 6.5–8s: Nagumo grips the table edge and holds
- 8–9s: dust settles on the papers; his eyes stop on the new report
Camera: 50mm, high angle tilting up to his face.
Light: window light through falling dust.
Sound: a dull blast, groaning steel, sifting dust.
Ends on: his eyes decided.

Shot 3 (9–16s) · CUT TO — DECISION
Refs: @image2, @image3, @image1, @image6
Frame: NAGUMO in front three-quarter medium close-up, the STAFF OFFICER on the right edge; behind, a firing AA tub through the window.

- 9–10.5s: he sweeps the Midway attack order aside with the back of his hand
- 10.5–11.5s: he slaps the new report flat with his palm
- 11.5–16s: he gives the order, resolved
Dialogue (NAGUMO, Japanese, resolved, rising) at 11.5–16s: "ミッドウェー攻撃隊を、魚雷と徹甲爆弾に再換装せよ！敵艦隊を撃滅する！"
Camera: 65mm, slow push-in.
Light: window side light, deep-set shadowed eyes.
Sound: paper sliding, the palm slap, the order, muffled guns.
Ends on: his mouth set hard.

Shot 4 (16–21s) · CUT TO — THE RISK
Refs: @image3, @image1

- 17.5–20s: he voices the risk, carefully
- 20–21s: a blast flashes in his lenses
Dialogue (STAFF OFFICER, Japanese, careful, hesitant) at 17.5–20s: "換装中に攻撃されたら……。"
Camera: 85mm, locked-off.
Light: the window reflected in his glasses.
Sound: hesitant voice, a blast.

Shot 5 (21–25s) · CUT TO — FIGHTERS — there!
Refs: @image2, @image1, @image9
